Buttons

*hitWall
  hidden
  shown
  name:Punch wall
  icon:https://i.imgur.com/108BtPH.png
  on click:
    yield 1 money
    yield 1 totalMoney
    yield 1 handDamage
  end

*makeBricks
  hidden
  name:Create Bricks
  icon:https://i.imgur.com/Tdh80P1.png
  on click:
    yield 1 bricks
    yield 1 totalBricks
    yield 1 handBricks
  end

*reset
  hidden
  name:Rewind time (Removes all buildings & resources, converts cosmic knowledge into prestige, will soon turn building damage into coins)
  on click:
    prestige is prestige+prePrestige
    prePrestige is 0
    timesWon is timesWon+goldBricks
    goldBricks is 0
    bonusBossPoint is bonusBossPoint+bonusBossBrick

    timesActuallyWon is timesActuallyWon+winBricks
    winBricks is 0
    meshBossPoint is meshBossPoint+meshBricks
    meshBricks is 0

*destroyWall
  hidden
  desc:Destroy the wall and move on to a bigger one.</>Gives <b>25</b> bricks.</>Awards <b>250,000,000,000</b> cash.</>Worth <b>1</b> cosmic knowledge.
  name:Destroy wall
  shown
  cost:1000000000000 totalMoney, 1 wallRequirement
  on earn:
    yield 250000000000 money
    yield 25 bricks
    yield 25 totalBricks
    hide destroyWall
    yield 1 wallRequirement
    show wd19
    show magicUnlock
    show wallMultiplier
    show destroyBigWall
    do wdsync
  end
  on wdsync:
    wallsDestroyed is wallsDestroyed+(this-wdrep1)*1
    prePrestige is prePrestige+(this-wdrep1)*1
    wdrep1 is this
  end
  on lose:
    do wdsync
  end

*destroyBigWall
  hidden
  desc:Destroy the big 2nd wall and move on to a bigger one.</>Gives <b>100</b> bricks.</>Awards <b>25,000,000,000,000</b> cash.</>Worth <b>1</b> cosmic knowledge.
  name:Destroy 2nd wall
  cost:100000000000000 totalMoney, 1 wallRequirement
  on earn:
    yield 25000000000000 money
    yield 100 bricks
    yield 100 totalBricks
    hide destroyBigWall
    yield 1 wallRequirement
    show wd20
    show magicLevel2
    show giantUnlock
    show destroyHugeWall
    do wdsync
  end
  on wdsync:
    wallsDestroyed is wallsDestroyed+(this-wdrep2)*1
    prePrestige is prePrestige+(this-wdrep2)*1
    wdrep2 is this
  end
  on lose:
    do wdsync
  end

  hidden

*destroyFinalWall
  hidden
  desc:Destroy the 5th and final wall.</>Gives <b>10,000,000</b> bricks.</>Awards <b>25,000,000,000,000,000,000</b> cash; <b>1</b> boss brick.</>Worth <b>5</b> cosmic knowledge.

  cost:100000000000000000000 totalMoney, 1 wallRequirement
  on earn:
    yield 25000000000000000000 money
    yield 10000000 bricks
    yield 10000000 totalBricks
    hide destroyFinalWall
    yield 1 wallRequirement

  on wdsync:
    goldBricks is goldBricks+(this-wdrep5)*1

    prePrestige is prePrestige+(this-wdrep5)*5
    wdrep5 is this
  end
  on lose:
    do wdsync
  end
